# Known facts

Only DOCUMENTED, INFERRED-with-high-confidence, OBSERVED or VERIFIED items. Each has a source.
Items marked OBSERVED come from experiments E1, E2, E3, E3b, E8, E8b, E9, E9b, E12 and E12b on the user's console (2026-10-28,
2026-10-29, 2026-10-30 and 2026-10-31, one PC). **The VERIFIED count is 74** (it was 2 until 2026-10-29, 26 until
the E3b pass, 35 until the E8 pass, 39 until the E8b pass, 49 until the E9 pass of 2026-10-30 and 31 and 53 until the E9b pass and 61 until the E12 pass of 2026-10-31 and 65 until the E12b pass of 2026-11-01 and 72 until the E11 pass of 2026-11-01, evening, and 73 until the E9c pass of 2026-11-02): F41 (product string), F48, after the independent E3 session (a second session
after a console power cycle, same PC) F51–F55 (F55 in part), F57–F65, F67 (for the IDs seen in both
sessions), F68 and F70–F77 ("the console returns these bodies"; the reading against the show file
stays OBSERVED), after the E3b sessions (three more sessions after another console power cycle,
same PC), nine facts first seen in E3, **each in part: the structural part only, never a live
value**: F80, F82–F87, F89 and F92, and, after E8 (the first live read-only session of our own software: a second host program on
the same PC, its own transfer log, no bus capture), F95 and, **each in part**, F99, F100 and F101 (the structural part only, never a live
value; F100: the LED pattern only, its identity as the SOLO key stays INFERRED), and, after E8b (the second read-only session of our own software, with the bus seen in parallel, a named backup and a timed action log), F110, F112, F113, F114 (the steady-blue pattern of block 53 only), F115 and F116, F119 and F120 and, **each in part**, F117 and F118 (the structural parts only, never a live value; the key identities of F115 and F116 are OBSERVED or INFERRED, not VERIFIED), and, after E9 (a SmartSoft session driven through the Console Simulator and recorded passively: the first capture of a PC writing to the console, separate from E3b), F93 (the console's counter is not reset by a new session), F94 (for its structure), F97 (for BUMPS 0xFF with SOLO off) and F103 (FileName follows the last SD save), each reproduced in a second session (E3b + E9; the structural parts only, never a live value), and, after E9b (the repeat of E9: three more SmartSoft sessions in one capture, recorded passively, 427 data writes), F131 (the write framing and the outbound counter), F132 (ConsoleEvent kind 0, the fader), F133 (kind 1, the key), F134 (the console's answer to PC events), F138 (the ChannelText write) and F140 (opening the Console Simulator sends nothing), each for the scope named in its row, and, **each in part**, F135 (a PC fader event takes effect at once) and F136 (REC MEM stores the look and the console lists the Memory object; an EDIT MEM selection of memory 1 lists System (5, 5, 0)) (E9 + E9b; as SmartSoft's and the console's behaviour, never clearance to send), and, after E12 (a passive capture of SmartSoft Online editing the patch, the independents, the stack, a sequence, a memory and page names: three more SmartSoft sessions after another console power cycle, a capture separate from E9b, [E12 record](captures/2026-10-31-e12-smartsoft-patch-stack-edits.md)), F143 (the Memory (0) write as SmartSoft sends it, and the console storing it) and F147 (the console lists the Memory object after a recording or a deletion made with its own keys) and, **each in part**, F144 (a ChannelText block written by SmartSoft is kept across a console power cycle and returned byte for byte; the write-to-next-download round trips stay OBSERVED) and F146 (REC MEM of an empty look empties a memory, from the console's own keys; the PC-key path stays OBSERVED) (E9b + E12; as SmartSoft's and the console's behaviour, never clearance to send), and, after E12b (the repeat of E12: two more SmartSoft sessions in one capture after another console power cycle, a capture separate from E12, [E12b record](captures/2026-10-31-e12b-smartsoft-patch-stack-edits-repeat.md)), F151 (the step commands as SmartSoft sends them and the console's lists after them), F152 (InsertStep's two phases and the console's default step), F156 (Sequence records with c = the changed step's index, and body lengths that follow the content) and F157 (the downloads around an edit session), each for the scope named in its row, and, **each in part**, F153 (the whole-object writes of the patch, the independents, steps, a sequence and pages: their layouts, the written object never listed back, the console storing what was written), F154 (the positions of IND 1's button-mode and GM bytes, the run mode 0 Loop / 1 One Shot, step type 2 = the console's "State") and F158 (SmartSoft's stack view sends live keys) (E12 + E12b; as SmartSoft's and the console's behaviour, never clearance to send). An
OBSERVED item becomes VERIFIED only by an independent reproduction on our unit (second session or
second PC, or a genuinely different evidence channel) with its own recorded evidence entry, as
defined in [README.md](README.md). A verbal confirmation never promotes. **VERIFIED never means
"we may send it":** it says that the console or SmartSoft was seen to behave that way in two
sessions; for F58 (the two System writes) it means that SmartSoft sends exactly these bytes at
these points. No PC -> console message is cleared for sending (safety rule 2 in
[reverse-engineering.md](reverse-engineering.md)), so **no code may send anything to a console
yet**. *(Superseded in part 2026-10-30 by safety check SC-1, [safety-checks.md](safety-checks.md): the poll and
the fetch of a listed record are cleared for a read-only session; nothing else is.)* *(Further superseded in part 2026-10-30 by E8, [record](captures/2026-10-30-e8-first-read-only-session.md): our own software has now run a read-only session of 861 s; E8 did not clear any other message, and VERIFIED is still necessary, not sufficient.)* *(Further superseded in part 2026-10-30, evening, by E8b, [record](captures/2026-10-30-e8b-second-read-only-session.md): a second read-only session of our own software, 1 121 s, with USBPcap running from before the Zadig bind; it cleared no further message either, and SC-1's poll and listed fetch remain the only messages cleared.)* *(Further superseded in part 2026-10-30, late evening, by E9, [record](captures/2026-10-30-e9-smartsoft-writes.md): the first capture of a PC writing to the console, SmartSoft's own session recorded passively; it observed the first write messages (ConsoleEvent faders and keys, a ChannelText label) and cleared none: they are OBSERVED in one session, a future safety check SC-2 would be their gate, and SC-1's poll and listed fetch remain the only messages cleared.)* *(Further superseded in part 2026-10-31 by E9b, [record](captures/2026-10-31-e9b-smartsoft-writes-repeat.md): the repeat of E9, three more SmartSoft sessions recorded passively, 427 data writes; it reproduced the write framing and the most-used ConsoleEvents, which makes them VERIFIED as SmartSoft's and the console's behaviour, and cleared no message: no type-1 data header is encoded anywhere in the solution *(Superseded 2026-10-31, later the same day: the owner implemented SC-2's whitelist in `HspOutbound` and `HspReadSession`, sent only by a session with live control switched on for the day (`Device:Usb:LiveControlDate`); reviewed under SC-2 item 10, NEEDS CHANGES at the first pass, see safety-checks.md; nothing is cleared for sending before E11 on the owner's go.)*, a future safety check SC-2 is the gate for every write, and SC-1's poll and listed fetch remain the only messages cleared.)* Everything first seen in E3b (F93–F106, except the ones promoted above), in E8 (F110–F120, except the ones promoted above; F111 stays OBSERVED), in E8b (F122–F130), in E9 (F131–F140, except the ones promoted above; F137 and F139 stay OBSERVED, F135 and F136 only in part), in E9b (F141–F147, except the ones promoted above; F141, F142 and F145 stay OBSERVED, F144 and F146 only in part), in E12 (F151–F159, except the ones promoted above; F155 and F159 stay OBSERVED, F153, F154 and F158 only in part) or in E12b (F160–F165, every one OBSERVED in one capture), and every part of an earlier fact not named above
(F81, F88, F90, F91, the live values, the sub-readings, the key-LED identities of the record keys, CLEAR and MAGIC (H72, INFERRED) and the readings of FaderInfo bytes 1–3 (H71, H69's per-fader reading now CONFIRMED in F127)), is OBSERVED at most (one session or one
capture, one PC, one show); an E8 reading that rests on the owner's untimed account or on the manual is marked INFERRED or HYPOTHESIS in its row. F5–F8 are DOCUMENTED (console manual, added 2026-10-29); F107 (HTP and the memory record / edit / page rules), F108 (independents: default patch DMX 97 / 98) and F109 (CLEAR), all console manual, added 2026-10-30 for the simulator, are DOCUMENTED and sit with them in the Hardware section. F121 (sending a show and the System erase commands are destructive paths) comes from static analysis and the SmartSoft manual (INFERRED / DOCUMENTED) and sits in the SmartSoft ↔ console link section. F148 and F149 (the console manual's patch functions, and its stack, sequence and run-mode rules) and F150 (SmartSoft's editing tabs), added 2026-10-31 for the planned capture E12, are DOCUMENTED, observed nowhere, and sit in the Hardware section (F148, F149) and the SmartSoft ↔ console link section (F150); the INFERRED static findings about the messages SmartSoft would send for such edits are in smartsoft-analysis.md S11.16 and the hypotheses H91–H101, not here, because nothing of them was ever observed. *(Superseded in part 2026-10-31, evening, by the E12 analysis, text kept: E12 saw the console's patch and stack menus of F148 and F149 on its LCD and used SmartSoft's tabs of F150 (annotations in the three rows); the messages SmartSoft sends for those edits are now OBSERVED once (F151–F159, section E12 below), and H91–H101 carry E12's verdicts.)*

## Hardware

| # | Fact | Status | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| F1 | The product is the ETC **SmartFade** 1248 (SF1248), not "SmartFace" | DOCUMENTED | Spec sheet, all manuals |
| F2 | Interfaces: DMX512 out (1 universe), DMX512 in, MIDI In, MIDI Out, USB (Series B device), SD card slot. No RS-232 and no Ethernet listed | DOCUMENTED | Spec sheet "Interfaces"; RTF spec D.1 |
| F3 | Normal mode: 48 channels, 288 memories (12 pages × 24), 48 sequences (12 × 4) of ≤ 24 steps, 199-step stack, 10 snapshots, 2 independents, GM, blackout, bumps master | DOCUMENTED | Spec sheet; RTF; console manual v3.0 |
| F4 | Operating modes Full Control / TwoScene / DMXBackup, selected at power-up | DOCUMENTED | Console manual v3.0 p.5 |
| F5 | **Master and Blackout.** "To get light output (at all), the Master needs to be at full, and the Black Out button off. The Master fader proportionally limits all intensity outputs. As soon as the Master is faded under 100% the Blackout button will blink. The Blackout button instantly sends all intensity outputs to zero. As soon as it is activated the button will light up." Intensities "are limited by the Master fader and Black out button". For intensity faders to control light output live the Master must be over zero, BLACKOUT off and NEXT off. IND 1 / IND 2 "are not affected by the Master fader or Black Out button unless they are set to "GM" in the Independent Settings". The panel label of the Master fader on the owner's 1248 is **MASTER**, next to a fader labelled **BUMPS**, with the INDEPENDENTS section below them (owner, 2026-10-29, Q5). The MIDI table of the same manual calls it "127 = Grand Master" (F6); the knowledge base writes **MASTER (Grand Master)** | DOCUMENTED (manual); OBSERVED (panel labels, owner statement) **E3b annotation 2026-10-30:** the Blackout LED blinked while the MASTER was below full (block 53, F85) and the live outputs were scaled by the MASTER (F96); both OBSERVED, the manual's quotes hold **E8 annotation 2026-10-30:** LedStatus block 53 read steady blue `00 00 FF 00 00 FF` three times while the owner had BLACKOUT on (INFERRED), over the MASTER blink, which returned once BLACKOUT was off with the MASTER still below full (F114): "the button will light up" holds as read; whether BLACKOUT sends the intensity outputs to zero in the level objects is untested (all levels were 0 in the three windows) **E8b annotation 2026-10-30:** "untested" is superseded (text kept): with channel fader 5 at full, BLACKOUT on set FaderInfo body[17], ChannelLevels[5], OutputLevels[6], LED block 5 G and the LCD cell to zero within 0.27 s and BLACKOUT off restored all five, the fixture went dark (owner), the MASTER staying at 0xFF (F125, seq 26987 … 27766): the manual's "instantly sends all intensity outputs to zero" is OBSERVED at the level objects; block 53 is steady blue while BLACKOUT is on, with timed keys (F114) | Console manual v3.0 (`SmartFade_v3.0_User_Manual_revA.pdf`, SHA-256 `1a93e0afcc18f26459249e5a8a3bb1582e6cfe8318559f4e7b748ff20580b414`, [source-materials.md](../source-materials.md) section 2; printed page = PDF page − 6): printed p.56 "Master & Black Out function" and its note (PDF p.62), p.12 "Faders & Bump buttons" (PDF p.18), p.30 "Intensity channels" (PDF p.36). Quotes re-checked against the PDF text (PyMuPDF) on 2026-10-29. Panel labels: [owner statements](captures/2026-10-29-e3-owner-statements.txt) Q5 |
| F6 | **Bump Master (the BUMPS fader) and bump modes.** "The Bump Master sets the level to which bump buttons will flash." "There are two Bump modes when a Bump button is pressed, Pile-on and Solo. Both use the level of the BUMPS fader. The SOLO button toggles between these modes." Memory and sequence faders: SOLO off = "The level generated by pressing a bump button adds into the live output", SOLO on = "replaces the current output". Intensity faders: "The bump buttons will bump instantly to the level of the BUMP fader. If SOLO mode is active the other intensity channels will be temporarily muted." MIDI controllers in the same manual: "126 = Bump Master", "127 = Grand Master". The knowledge base writes **BUMPS (bump master)** | DOCUMENTED **E3b annotation 2026-10-30:** BUMPS is FaderInfo body[193] (F95); a held bump put the BUMPS level (tested at 0xFF) into the level objects (F97); SOLO on and off looked alike with one output active (add versus replace untested) | Console manual v3.0: printed p.56 "Bump Master" (PDF p.62), p.12 "Bump Playback Modes" (PDF p.18), p.30 "Bumping channels" (PDF p.36), p.57 "Memory Bump Modes" (PDF p.63), p.58 "Sequence Bump Modes" (PDF p.64), p.27 "Basic MIDI implementation" (PDF p.33; same numbers as [midi-spec-analysis.md](midi-spec-analysis.md)) |
| F7 | **Physical faders and fader modes.** "SmartFade 1248 and 1296 look exactly like this 2496 console, the only difference being that they have two rows of 12 faders instead of two rows of 24 faders": the 1248 has **24 physical faders**. "There are 24 (48) faders. They can be set to control intensities or memories and sequences. These modes are set with the FADERS buttons." The parenthesis is the larger console: "12 fader pages with 24 (48) memory faders in each page … a possible total of 288 memories in a SmartFade 1248/1296 and 576 memories in a SmartFade 2496"; "There are 48 (96) intensity channels … from these two fader modes". The figures label the FADERS buttons 1-24, 25-48, 1-48 (and 49-96); the reading that a 1248 has the modes 1-24 and 25-48 for its 48 channels is INFERRED (the figure labels are not per model). Sequences "can be recorded to and played back from the last four faders", "Each fader page carries up to 4 sequences" (12 pages: 48 sequences) | DOCUMENTED (the quoted statements); INFERRED (the 1-24 / 25-48 reading) **E3b annotation 2026-10-30:** the bank reading is now OBSERVED on our unit: the owner's 'bank 25-48' press changed ConsoleStatus body[4] from 0 to 1 and the LCD row labels to '25' / '37' and made physical fader 1 move channel 25 (F98, F99); the 24 physical faders map to FaderInfo records 1–24 in every mode (F99) | Console manual v3.0: printed p.11 "Frontpanel Functionality" (PDF p.17), p.12 (PDF p.18), p.10 "Control Capacity" (PDF p.16), p.32 "Memories" (PDF p.38), p.36 "Sequences" (PDF p.42), p.58 (PDF p.64) |
| F8 | **Bump-button LEDs and the LCD.** "Each fader has a Bump button below it. This button will light up when there is content, and it will mimic the output of the content by varying its intensity. The button color will vary depending on the type of content." Intensity faders: "The bump button under each fader will light up proportionally to the actual intensity of the corresponding outputs, regardless of the position of the fader. It will do so even if there are no outputs patched to the fader. The LCD will show the current output of the selected fader mode, with bar graphs." If a fader's position does not match its output "the bump button will blink until the actual level is matched by that of the fader"; Next mode speaks of "the green channel bumps". Memories: "A recorded memory is indicated in red. The bump button lights up at full if the fader is above zero, contributing to the light output"; in MEMS mode buttons with stored memories "light up dimmed in red … When the fader is contributing to the output, the button will light up in full". Sequences: "Memory faders containing sequences have yellow bump buttons", "dimmed in yellow … in full" when contributing | DOCUMENTED **E3b annotation 2026-10-30:** the owner's colours match: recorded memories red and bright, empty slots red and dim, the sequence slot yellowish (log 634.39 s; F84, H55); the bump LED of a held memory bump is full red (`FF 00 00`, F97) | Console manual v3.0: printed p.12 (PDF p.18), p.30 "Intensity mimic" and "Matching values" (PDF p.36), p.32 (PDF p.38), p.36 (PDF p.42), p.57 (PDF p.63), p.58 (PDF p.64), p.63 "Single Scene Crossfading" (PDF p.69) |
| F107 | **HTP, memory recording, editing and pages (console behaviour, used by the simulator).** HTP: "Dimmer channels operate using Highest Takes Precedence (HTP). This means that if an intensity is output from more than one fader, the highest level will be the resulting output." "Intensities are piled-on to the output of the console in an HTP manner (Highest Takes Precedence). Intensities may be sourced by a channel fader, a memory fader or the Stack. They are limited by the Master fader and Black out button." Memories: "There are 12 fader pages with 24 (48) memory faders in each page." REC MEM: "Set up a look you want to record using the channel faders. You may record the output from other memory faders as well." "Buttons that can record in this mode will blink at full. If there is a memory stored already, this button is dimmed (press CLEAR to exit)." "The moment the bump button is pressed, the memory is stored and the console will automatically return to it's previous fader mode. Press UNDO to undo this recording." EDIT MEM: "Every memory can be edited blind (fader at zero) or live (fader at full)." "All buttons with content that can be edited will blink at full (press CLEAR to exit)." "The moment the bump button is pressed, this memory is selected for editing. EDIT MEM and CLEAR will blink." "As soon as EDIT MEM is pressed the changes are stored. The console will automatically return to it's previous fader mode. If you press CLEAR instead, changes are aborted." "To delete a single memory edit it so all intensities are at zero." Pages: "If a fader is above zero when a different page is selected, it will not be updated to the content of the new page until it is brought to zero." Not stated by the manual and therefore INFERRED where the simulator needs them: proportional playback of a memory by its fader, recording before the MASTER, held bumps excluded from a recording | DOCUMENTED (the quotes); INFERRED (the simulator's readings named above). Console behaviour, not protocol knowledge: no message is involved **E9 annotation 2026-10-30 (late evening; OBSERVED once, from key events of SmartSoft's Simulator):** the console behaved as the quotes say: REC MEM and a bump stored the current look in the pressed bump's slot and the console returned to its previous fader mode (key 0x41 then 0x08: Memory (a = 0, b = 8) with channel 5 = 0x79, listed by the console itself); EDIT MEM and a bump selected a memory for a blind edit (channel mode, LCD "Edit:" / "01/09"); a second EDIT MEM stored (the Memory object was listed); CLEAR aborted ("If you press CLEAR instead, changes are aborted" holds); COPY, a source bump and a target bump wrote the target (F136). "To delete a single memory edit it so all intensities are at zero" was not exercised: EDIT MEM, a bump and CLEAR started from the stored levels and deleted nothing; the owner's own method, REC MEM with nothing raised (a statement), is H86. Recording before the MASTER (the simulator's INFERRED reading, H85) was not separated (the MASTER stood at full). "No message is involved" describes the manual's text: the same functions were driven by key events in E9 (F133). The quotes stay DOCUMENTED **E9b annotation 2026-10-31 (OBSERVED once; the status line above is unchanged for the manual's quotes): the simulator's reading "REC MEM records before the MASTER" (INFERRED) is contradicted.** Channel fader 5 stood at 0x7C and the console's MASTER at 0x86; REC MEM and bump 9 from the PC stored channel 5 = **0x41** (Memory (0, 0, 8) = `00 08 01 60` + channel 5 = 0x41, seq 79066, f158132), the post-MASTER level the console reported for the channel (0x7C × 0x86 / 255 = 65.2; F96, F145); H85 REJECTED. The manual's wording ("Set up a look you want to record", the current look) fits: with the MASTER at full (E9's memory 9, B7) the two readings cannot be told apart. REC MEM and a bump with nothing raised **emptied** the memory (body[2] = 0x00, 96 zero levels, listed at once; PC keys seq 84072 / 84076, console keys seq 98688 / 98692; the key shows empty, `0A 00 00 0A 00 00`, seq 85061): H86 CONFIRMED (F146), the owner's method; the manual's route "edit it so all intensities are at zero" (EDIT MEM) was not exercised in E9b (E9: EDIT MEM, a bump and CLEAR deleted nothing, F136). **The simulator was changed on 2026-10-31 (commit ac00b9e) to record the post-MASTER level, following F145 (OBSERVED once); the exclusion of held bumps from the stored look stays INFERRED, and the effect of BLACKOUT and SOLO on the stored look is UNKNOWN.** Whether a recorded memory plays back proportionally is still INFERRED. | Console manual v3.0: printed p.10 "Control Capacity" (PDF p.16), p.15 "HTP control rules" (PDF p.21), p.30 "Intensity channels" (PDF p.36), p.32 "Memories" and "Record a memory" (PDF p.38), p.44 "Edit Memories" (PDF p.50), p.57 "Memories" and "Select Fader Page" (PDF p.63). Used by `SmartFade.Devices.Simulated` and the UI (2026-10-30) **E9b:** seq 76489, 76687, 79062 / 79066, 84072 / 84076, 85061, 98688 / 98692 ([E9b record](captures/2026-10-31-e9b-smartsoft-writes-repeat.md) "The record functions (H85, H86, B7)"); F145, F146 |
| F108 | **Independents: default patch and behaviour.** "Independent buttons IND 1 and IND 2 provide two outputs separated from all other controls. They are intended to operate devices such as smoke machines, tab tracks, cue lights, etc." "SOLO does not affect these outputs." "The state or result of the 2 independent outputs is not recordable." "SmartFade defaults to the outputs 97 & 98 for the independents. IND 1 = DMX 97, IND 2 = DMX 98." "It is also possible to create a custom patch by assigning any DMX outputs to the independents. This is done in the Patch. They are indicated in the patch as channels i1 and i2." ## SmartSoft ↔ console link

| # | Fact | Status | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| F10 | SmartSoft connects to SmartFade consoles over **USB** and needs an ETC driver. Without it the console shows as "Unrecognized USB device". With it the device is "USB High-Speed-Pipe" | DOCUMENTED | SmartSoft manual 3.0.1 p.4 |
| F11 | The ETC driver (`etchsp.sys`, "High-Speed-Pipe", by Peperoni-Light) binds USB `VID_14D5` with PIDs `0103, 0110, 0112, 0200, 0201` (+`FFFB` on 64-bit lists), **interface `MI_01`** | INFERRED (driver INF content, high) | `etchsp.inf` in installer |
| F12 | The SmartSoft application and its SmartFade engine DLL import no Windows MIDI (winmm), DirectMusic or serial-port APIs | INFERRED (import tables, high) | `pe_report.py` on SmartSoft.exe, sfEngine.dll |
| F13 | `sfEngine.dll` opens a device by SetupAPI interface path and uses ReadFile/WriteFile/DeviceIoControl. Its strings reference HighSpeedPipeline, Poll, Fetch object, DirtyObjects, timeouts and retries | INFERRED (imports + strings, high) | smartsoft-analysis.md S4, S6 |
| F14 | `sfEngine.dll` selects the console by enumerating device-interface GUID `{8D7140C7-BCAE-4BE2-A5C2-DFE48EBB759A}` (interface index 0 and 1 only) and keeping the interface whose `DeviceIoControl(0x222000)` returns PID **0x0200**; IOCTLs 0x222004 (BcdDevice) and 0x222008 (product string) are also issued. There is no VID comparison in the engine | INFERRED (hand-read disassembly + Ghidra pseudocode agree, high; not observed on hardware) | smartsoft-analysis.md S11.10 and S11.14.10, 2026-10-16 |
| F15 | `sfEngine.dll` identifies protocol objects by an 8-bit ID that indexes a 42-entry name table (0 Memory … 20 ConsoleEvent … 36 FileName, 37 ConsoleVersion, 38 SystemSizes, 40 DirtyObjects, 41 SystemSettings; full list S11.6). Receive handlers for IDs 40, 36, 37 and 38 behave as their names suggest. ID 42 is used by code but has no table entry | INFERRED (hand-read disassembly + Ghidra pseudocode and table bytes agree, high) | smartsoft-analysis.md S11.6 and S11.14.6 |
| F16 | SmartSoft requires console Normal mode and a console firmware version matching SmartSoft | INFERRED (UI strings, medium-high) | SmartSoft.exe strings; manual p.5 |
| F17 | SmartSoft 3.0.2b package contains SmartSoft, SmartFade and SmartFade ML software, all version 3.0.2.9.0.43 | DOCUMENTED | `Setup Information.rtf` |
| F121 | **Destructive write paths (static): sending a show and the System erase commands.** SmartSoft can replace the console's show, and System (ID 39) carries erase commands. (1) "New show" (after the dialog "This will erase all data, OK to proceed?") and "Open showfile" end, when Online, in the full upload of the engine's show (tail jumps `0x10018503` and `0x100185E1`); "Save As" sends sendObject(36 FileName) when Online (`0x10018751`); **sending a show replaces the console's show.** (2) After their confirmation dialogs the System-tab dispatcher `0x446092` sends System (ID 39) with a = 11 "Erase memories" (`0x446657`), 12 "Erase sequences" (`0x446726`), 13 "Erase stack" (`0x4467F5`), 14 "Erase palettes" (`0x4468C4`) or 16 "Erase all" (`0x44698F`), all through the shared send at `0x44699A`–`0x4469A3`; the mapping fits the mirror parser's a ∈ {10, 11, 12, 13, 16}; the console's effect is UNKNOWN. (3) GUI edits of single objects (labels, memories, patch, independents, settings, steps) are whole-object writes of the engine's full copy (serializer `0x10015E50`). None of this was seen on the bus: SmartSoft sent only System `09 00 00` and `07 00 00` in E2, E3 and E3b, and E8 sent only polls and listed fetches. **Rule for our code: never send a show, never send System with a = 11, 12, 13, 14 or 16, and never trigger either path (the SD card is the non-USB way to load a show).** SC-1 clears neither, and no type-1 data header is encoded anywhere in the solution *(Superseded 2026-10-31, later the same day: the owner implemented SC-2's whitelist in `HspOutbound` and `HspReadSession`, sent only by a session with live control switched on for the day (`Device:Usb:LiveControlDate`); reviewed under SC-2 item 10, NEEDS CHANGES at the first pass, see safety-checks.md; nothing is cleared for sending before E11 on the owner's go.)* | **DOCUMENTED** (sending a show replaces the console's show: SmartSoft manual 3.0.1 p. 24, "If a console is connected the same show will be loaded to the console. Make sure you have saved your data before loading or connecting."). **INFERRED, high** (the code path of the show upload; the erase mapping from the dialogs' texts); INFERRED, medium (whole-object writes). Static analysis only, not observed on the bus; the console's effect of the erase commands is UNKNOWN **E9 annotation 2026-10-30 (late evening):** the first capture of a PC writing to the console shows **none of the destructive paths**: of the 401 data writes none is a show upload (no Open showfile, New show or Save As was used), a FileName write or a System erase (a = 11, 12, 13, 14, 16); the only System writes are the `09 00 00` / `07 00 00` pair (no page select, H51 untested). The Mem Edit tab's whole-object Memory write (S11.15 row 6) was not exercised: both Mem Edit steps went through the console's EDIT MEM by Simulator key and fader events (the owner, 2026-10-31: "From what I remember I used console simulator for mem edit", a recollection that agrees with the capture and promotes nothing). The static readings above are unchanged and the rule for our code stands **E9b annotation 2026-10-31:** E9b's 427 data writes again contain **no show upload, no FileName write and no System erase** (a = 11, 12, 13, 14, 16), no page select and nothing on interface 0; the System writes are the `09 00 00` / `07 00 00` pair × 3. **The Mem Edit tab's whole-object Memory write, which E9 did not exercise, was exercised once (F143):** a 90-byte Memory (0) body for memory 9 of page 1, accepted by the console; it replaces one memory, not the show, is show data and is not reversible by the protocol without the exact old body, and it is not cleared (H87). The rule for our code stands. **E12 annotation 2026-10-31, evening:** E12's 70 writes contain no show upload, no FileName write, no System erase and no page select; its System writes are the pair × 3. **Reading (3) is now OBSERVED once** for the patch, the independents, stack steps, a sequence, pages and memories (F153; the Memory write first in E9b, F143): whole-object writes of SmartSoft's copy, nothing listed back, stored by the console, each reversible only by another exact write or by the SD backup; settings were not exercised (never, by the owner's decision). The rule for our code stands. | [smartsoft-analysis.md](smartsoft-analysis.md) S11.15 rows 6–8 (`sfEngine.dll` 3228bd9b…7983, `SmartSoft.exe` 4c4c9cf7…8c01, disassembly and strings, 2026-10-30); SmartSoft user manual 3.0.1 p. 24 (`smartsoft-source-materials/SmartSoft_v3.0.1_User_Manual_revA.pdf`); [safety-checks.md](safety-checks.md) (scope of SC-1); H5, H6, H24 **E9b:** the inventory of the 427 writes ([E9b record](captures/2026-10-31-e9b-smartsoft-writes-repeat.md) "Inventory of the PC → console writes") **E12:** the inventory of the 70 writes ([E12 record](captures/2026-10-31-e12-smartsoft-patch-stack-edits.md) "Inventory of the PC → console writes"); F153 |
| F150 | **SmartSoft's editing tabs (SmartSoft manual 3.0.1).** Playback tab: "click on a text area to edit a name"; in the cue list zone "Click on a step time in the Stack to edit." (printed p.12). Page tab: "You can set/edit names for each Page, memory and sequence. Click on names to edit." (printed p.14). ## Our console: hardware evidence (E1, 2026-10-28)

Source for every row: [captures/2026-10-28-e1-usb-identification.md](captures/2026-10-28-e1-usb-identification.md)
and the evidence files named there. Host: owner's PC, Windows 11 Pro build 26300, SmartSoft
not running.

| # | Fact | Status | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| F40 | The console enumerates as `USB\VID_14D5&PID_0200&REV_0103`, a USB composite device (`usbccgp`). Device descriptor `12 01 10 01 00 00 00 40 D5 14 00 02 03 01 01 02 00 01`: USB 1.10, **full speed**, EP0 max packet 64, bcdDevice 0x0103, 1 configuration, iManufacturer 1, iProduct 2, **no serial number** (iSerialNumber 0) | OBSERVED (the archived run; a first run minutes earlier showed the same bytes but was overwritten) | `2026-10-28-e1-pnputil.txt`, `2026-10-28-e1-descriptors.txt`, `2026-10-28-e1-pnp-properties.txt` (service names). **Second sighting 2026-10-28 (E2):** the device descriptor `12 01 10 01 00 00 00 40 D5 14 00 02 03 01 01 02 00 01` is byte-identical in E2 (f2, JSONL seq 1) after a reboot, now at USB address 10 (E1: 50). Not counted as a reproduction: the frame is a synthetic copy that `USBPcapCMD --inject-descriptors` writes at t = 0 from the hub's cached descriptor (IRP ID 0), the same mechanism as the E1 descriptor dump. Status stays OBSERVED ([E2 record](captures/2026-10-28-e2-smartsoft-connect.md)). **Third sighting 2026-10-29 (E3):** the device descriptor is again byte-identical (f2, JSONL seq 1), now after a console power cycle and a re-enumeration at USB address 7. It is again a synthetic `--inject-descriptors` frame (`"synthetic":true`, IRP ID 0), the same mechanism as the E1 dump and the E2 frame, so it is no independent channel: status stays OBSERVED ([E3 record](captures/2026-10-29-e3-console-actions.md) "Reproduction of E2") **Fourth sighting 2026-10-30 (E3b):** the device descriptor is byte-identical again (f2) at USB address 7 and is again a synthetic `--inject-descriptors` frame, so it is no independent channel: status stays OBSERVED ([E3b record](captures/2026-10-30-e3b-live-objects.md)) **Sighting in E9 (2026-10-30, late evening):** the device descriptor `12 01 10 01 00 00 00 40 D5 14 00 02 03 01 01 02 00 01` is byte-identical again (f2, JSONL seq 1, USB address 10) and is again a synthetic `--inject-descriptors` frame (`"synthetic":true`), as in E8b, so it is no independent channel: status stays OBSERVED ([E9 record](captures/2026-10-30-e9-smartsoft-writes.md) "Session health") **Sighting in E9b (2026-10-31):** the device descriptor `12 01 10 01 00 00 00 40 D5 14 00 02 03 01 01 02 00 01` is byte-identical again (f1 / f2, JSONL seq 1, USB address 21 after the console's power cycle; seq 1–3 of the E9b and E9 JSONL compared by the curator, read-only) and is again a synthetic `--inject-descriptors` frame (`"synthetic":true`), as in E3b, E8b and E9, so it is no independent channel: status stays OBSERVED ([E9b record](captures/2026-10-31-e9b-smartsoft-writes-repeat.md) "Session health") |
| F41 | USB strings: manufacturer "ETC", product "ETC SmartFade" (bus-reported device description and HID product string agree) | **Product string: VERIFIED 2026-10-28.** Manufacturer string "ETC": OBSERVED (E1 only) | E1: `2026-10-28-e1-hid-probe.txt`, `2026-10-28-e1-pnp-properties.txt` (`DEVPKEY_Device_BusReportedDeviceDesc`). **E2 reproduction:** non-synthetic control transfers on the bus at 28.47 s, GET_DESCRIPTOR(STRING 0) answered `04 03 09 04` (f8, seq 4) and GET_DESCRIPTOR(STRING 2, LANGID 0x0409) answered `1C 03 45 00 54 00 43 00 20 00 53 00 6D 00 61 00 72 00 74 00 46 00 61 00 64 00 65 00` = "ETC SmartFade" (f10, seq 5, status 0). Reasoning: E1 saw the string through Windows APIs (HID product string, bus-reported description); E2 is the device's own answer on the wire, captured by USBPcap in a different session after a reboot. That is a different channel with its own recorded entry. Limit: E2 shows no read of string index 1, so "ETC" (manufacturer) is not reproduced ([E2 record](captures/2026-10-28-e2-smartsoft-connect.md)). **Third read 2026-10-29 (E3, after a console power cycle):** non-synthetic control transfers on the bus at 6.24 s again answered GET_DESCRIPTOR(STRING 0) `04 03 09 04` (f8, seq 4) and GET_DESCRIPTOR(STRING 2, 0x0409) with the same 28 bytes = "ETC SmartFade" (f10, seq 5, status 0); string index 1 ("ETC") was not read again ([E3 record](captures/2026-10-29-e3-console-actions.md)) **E3b (2026-10-30), three SmartSoft sessions after another power cycle:** non-synthetic control transfers answered GET_DESCRIPTOR(STRING 2, 0x0409) with the same 28 bytes = "ETC SmartFade" in every session (f10, f5386, f10084); GET_DESCRIPTOR(STRING 0) `04 03 09 04` only in session 1 (f8); string index 1 ("ETC") was not read again ([E3b record](captures/2026-10-30-e3b-live-objects.md)) **E8b (2026-10-30), our own software over WinUSB, with the bus:** the console answered two non-synthetic GET_DESCRIPTOR(STRING 2, 0x0409) reads with the same text, "ETC SmartFade" (wLength 4: `1C 03 45 00`, f9 → f11; wLength 28: `1C 03 45 00 54 00 43 00 …`, f12 → f16), at the Zadig bind, 20:36:45, 78 s before our first poll; the component that issued the reads is not visible (INFERRED: Windows or the restarted function drivers after the driver change; our code issues no control transfer, F123). Windows user-mode read buffers are `InputReportByteLength` = 65 bytes; byte 0 is the report-ID byte, which is 0x00 when the descriptor defines no IDs (DOCUMENTED Windows HID behaviour, not yet seen in a received report). **Rule:** our tools never send HID output or feature reports; an enabling message may only be reproduced after it was observed from SmartSoft (not SFDownloader) and understood | OBSERVED (capabilities) | `2026-10-28-e1-hid-probe.txt` |

## Our console: SmartSoft session over USB (E2, 2026-10-28)

Source for every row: [captures/2026-10-28-e2-smartsoft-connect.md](captures/2026-10-28-e2-smartsoft-connect.md)
(record), the committed console-only capture `2026-10-28-e2-smartsoft-connect.pcap` and its
normalization `2026-10-28-e2-smartsoft-connect.jsonl` (JSONL `seq`; `fN` = pcap frame number).
Host: owner's PC, Windows 11 Pro build 26300, Memory Integrity off, `etchsp.sys` 2.00.000
loaded (F49), SmartSoft 3.0.43 (package 3.0.2.9.0.43b), USBPcap 1.5.4.0; console firmware
3.0.2.9.0.43. Passive capture: nothing was sent by us. **All rows are OBSERVED (one session,
one PC) unless stated; none is VERIFIED.** *(That is the 2026-10-28 wording, kept. **Update
2026-10-29:** the independent E3 session ([record](captures/2026-10-29-e3-console-actions.md);
second session after a console power cycle, same PC, USB address 7) reproduced most of these rows.
The Status column now says which rows are VERIFIED, with the E3 frames in the Source column;
rows E3 did not reproduce stay OBSERVED. VERIFIED here means reproduced behaviour, not clearance
for sending.)* Labels w0..w5 / seq / id / body are the S11 and
H15–H20 vocabulary, not names from ETC. Nothing here may be sent to a console (no
`/hardware-safety-check` has passed). *(SC-1 passed on 2026-10-30 for the poll and the fetch of a listed
record, read-only session, [safety-checks.md](safety-checks.md); nothing else in this section is cleared.)*

| F51 | **Transport.** The SmartSoft session runs on interface 1 only: bulk OUT 0x04 (3 233 URBs) and bulk IN 0x83 (3 245 URBs), 6 478 data-carrying URBs in 28.478–125.253 s, no gap > 100 ms. The driver issues one bulk URB per `WriteFile` / `ReadFile`: the 12-byte header and the payload are separate URBs (INs up to 792 bytes are single URBs). Every reply-header IN directly follows a poll OUT, and every payload IN follows its own reply-header IN (244 payload INs in E2): the console never sends unsolicited data (0 exceptions in 3 245) | **VERIFIED 2026-10-29** (E2 + E3; was OBSERVED). The URB counts quoted are E2's **E8b annotation 2026-10-30 (the status stands; one sentence of the Fact cell is limited, text kept):** "The driver issues one bulk URB per `WriteFile` / `ReadFile` … INs up to 792 bytes are single URBs" describes **the ETC driver** of E2, E3 and E3b. Under **WinUSB** (our host, E8b, seen on the bus) every IN read longer than 64 bytes and not a multiple of 64 is two URBs, a whole-packet part and the remainder (1 845 reads, F122); every OUT and every IN of up to 64 bytes is one URB. The split is host-side (INFERRED); the console sees IN tokens either way, so the transport rows of this fact (interface 1 only, bulk 0x04 / 0x83, header and payload as separate transfers, nothing unsolicited) are unaffected | E2 record "Structure"; JSONL seq 6 onward. **E3 reproduction (second session after a console power cycle, USB address 7):** interface 1 only, bulk OUT 0x04 / IN 0x83, one URB per transfer (10 119 OUT and 10 202 IN URBs in 6.250–313.337 s, no gap > 100 ms), 0 of 10 202 IN transfers unsolicited, INs up to 792 bytes as single URBs (f352, seq 176); [E3 record](captures/2026-10-29-e3-console-actions.md) "Structure" **E3b (2026-10-30), three sessions after another power cycle:** interface 1 only, bulk OUT 0x04 (26 836 URBs) and IN 0x83 (27 092 URBs), 0 URBs on EP 0x81 / 0x02, every IN transfer solicited ([E3b record](captures/2026-10-30-e3b-live-objects.md) "Structure") **E8 (2026-10-30, our own software, no USBPcap):** reproduced by our own host, the first evidence channel other than SmartSoft (no status change): interface 1 only, bulk OUT 0x04 (23 688 transfers) and IN 0x83 (26 158), every IN solicited; our code never opened interface 0, which no bus capture confirms ([E8 record](captures/2026-10-30-e8-first-read-only-session.md) "Session health against SC-1") **E8b (2026-10-30, our own software with USBPcap, the first bus view of our host):** interface 1 only: 62 957 bulk URBs, 29 799 OUT on 0x04 (all 12-byte headers) and 33 158 IN on 0x83 (31 313 reads, the surplus being WinUSB's split reads), every reply header following a poll (27 484 of 27 484) and every payload following its header; **the bus confirms that the HID interface carried no data**: nothing on 0x02, no interrupt IN data on 0x81 (four interrupt URBs in all, at the Zadig bind: two completed CANCELED and two resubmitted that never completed, F123) and no transfer of ours on interface 0 (the HID class SET_IDLE of that bind is on EP0, stalled, and came 11 s before the backend even started, so it was not ours) (f19 … f125932 of the console-only split; [E8b record](captures/2026-10-30-e8b-second-read-only-session.md) "USBPcap: the bus view of our session"; F122) |
| F52 | **PC → console header.** Every OUT transfer starts as a 12-byte header of six little-endian u16 words w0..w5. w0 = 0 (poll, 3 001), 1 (data, 2), 2 (fetch, 228): no other values in 3 231 headers. w1 = 0 for polls and fetches, = 7 for both data headers (followed by exactly 7 bytes). For fetches w2 = object ID, w3 / w4 / w5 = qualifiers a / b / c. In polls and data headers w2..w5 are stale: in all 3 002 such headers after the first they equal w2..w5 of the most recent fetch or reply header (example: poll f19 repeats fetch f17's ID 0x11); the very first poll f11 is all zero | **VERIFIED 2026-10-29** (E2 + E3; was OBSERVED) **E9 annotation 2026-10-30 (late evening; no new promotion):** the stale-word rule holds for the type-1 data headers too: all **401 of 401** data headers of E9 carry w2..w5 of the most recent fetch or reply header, and so do 52 858 of 52 858 poll and data headers after the first poll (`poll_rules.py`, `summary`); example: header seq 16559 `01 00 08 00 0D 00 00 00 00 00 00 00` after an OutputLevels reply (w2 = 13). The data headers' w1 is 7 (System and key events), 8 (fader events) or 43 (ChannelText) in E9, so "w1 = 7 for both data headers" describes E2, E3 and E3b (F131). SmartSoft's own bytes; no message is cleared **E9b annotation 2026-10-31 (the status line above is unchanged):** the stale-word rule held for 50 342 of 50 342 polls and for **427 of 427 data headers** (50 768 of 50 768 poll and data headers after the first poll); the data headers' w1 is 7 (System and key events), 8 (fader events), 43 (ChannelText) and **94 (the Memory (0) write, OBSERVED once, F143)**, so "w1 = 7 for both data headers" describes E2, E3 and E3b only. ## Our console: actions on the console while SmartSoft is Online (E3, 2026-10-29)

Source for every row: [captures/2026-10-29-e3-console-actions.md](captures/2026-10-29-e3-console-actions.md)
(record), the committed console-only capture `2026-10-29-e3-console-actions.pcap` and its
normalization `2026-10-29-e3-console-actions.jsonl` (JSONL `seq`; `fN` = pcap frame number of the
split), the owner's action log `2026-10-29-e3-console-actions.log` (merged into the JSONL as `note`
records) and the owner's statements of the same evening,
[captures/2026-10-29-e3-owner-statements.txt](captures/2026-10-29-e3-owner-statements.txt)
(SHA-256 `d3e0ee5b995e3e419d2fa96568644d94cb4a9599309a16f7b73f6c4d5dbde328`), cited below as Q1–Q5 /
A1–A4. The owner's words are statements: OBSERVED for what the owner saw; Claude's readings are
INFERRED; a verbal confirmation never promotes. Host as in E2 (owner's PC, Windows 11 Pro build
26300, Memory Integrity off, `etchsp.sys` 2.00.000, SmartSoft 3.0.43, USBPcap 1.5.4.0), console
firmware 3.0.2.9.0.43, now **after a console power cycle** (USB address 7). Passive capture: nothing
was sent by us. **All rows are OBSERVED (one session) unless stated.** "LED block k" = LedStatus
body[1 + 6(k − 1) .. 6k], numbered from 1, as in the E3 record. Panel names follow the owner's labels
(Q5) and the console manual (F5, F6): **MASTER (Grand Master)** and **BUMPS (bump master)**. "CL-ML"
marks a claim of the third-party ConsoleLink program about the SmartFade **ML** (evidence class 5,
never a 1248 fact). Nothing here may be sent to a console.

## Our console: three more SmartSoft sessions and live objects (E3b, 2026-10-30)

Source for every row: [captures/2026-10-30-e3b-live-objects.md](captures/2026-10-30-e3b-live-objects.md)
(record), the committed console-only capture `2026-10-30-e3b-live-objects.pcap` and its
normalization `2026-10-30-e3b-live-objects.jsonl` (JSONL `seq`; `fN` = pcap frame number of the
split), the owner's action log `2026-10-30-e3b-live-objects.log` (merged into the JSONL as `note`
records; it also holds the owner's answers, cited by capture-relative seconds or by log time) and the
owner's statements of the same day,
[captures/2026-10-30-e3b-owner-statements.txt](captures/2026-10-30-e3b-owner-statements.txt)
(SHA-256 `eb764fa7f576c4979d26725187abeb328dab0f3c5da1fc61e2d9b4e7eb4466c4`), cited below as E3b-Q1..Q3 /
E3b-A1..A3 (the E3 statements keep their names Q1–Q5 / A1–A4). The owner's words are statements:
OBSERVED for what the owner saw or did; Claude's readings are INFERRED; a verbal confirmation never
promotes. Host as in E2 and E3 (owner's PC, Windows 11 Pro build 26300, Memory Integrity off,
`etchsp.sys` 2.00.000, SmartSoft 3.0.43, USBPcap 1.5.4.0), console firmware 3.0.2.9.0.43, now **after
another console power cycle** (USB address 7). Three SmartSoft sessions were started by the guided
script `tools/usb-probe/e3b-guided-session.ps1` (SHA-256
`EF95D9C1D5EA4D86F6953042E6B4FC1F978564441F86F0265F9838543F3F7855`) with no power cycle between them; the
show was backed up first (`SHOW05.ASC`, F104). Passive capture: nothing was sent by us. **All rows
are OBSERVED (one capture) unless stated.** Notation as in the E3 section: "LED block k" = LedStatus
body[1 + 6(k − 1) .. 6k], "FaderInfo record N" = body[1 + 4(N − 1) .. 4N], both numbered from 1; times
are capture-relative seconds. Panel names: **MASTER (Grand Master)**, **BUMPS (bump master)**. "CL-ML"
marks a claim of the third-party ConsoleLink program about the SmartFade **ML** (evidence class 5,
never a 1248 fact). The owner's rig is one LED bar with four cells at DMX 5–16
([hardware doc](../hardware/smartfade-1248.md), "Owner's test rig"); the owner's log calls the cells
"fixtures". **Reproductions (no new F-number):** E3b reproduced F40–F42 (descriptors: synthetic
frames again, no promotion), F51–F65, F67, F68 and F70–F77 in three more sessions after another power
cycle; the show objects are byte-identical to E3's (`startdiff` E3 versus E3b: 156 of the 161 shared
object keys identical; the 5 that differ are FaderInfo and DisplayContent (live state), FileName,
SystemSettings and the first DirtyObjects list; InputLevels appears only in E3, PlaybackInfo only in
E3b), and every such row now carries its E3b evidence in its Source cell. **VERIFIED never means that we
may send a message: no PC -> console message is cleared, and no code may send anything to a console.** *(SC-1, 2026-10-30: the poll and the listed fetch are now cleared for a read-only session, see [safety-checks.md](safety-checks.md); this sentence stands for every other message.)*

(record), the committed JSONL `2026-10-30-e8-first-read-only-session.jsonl` (the backend's own transfer log,

byte-identical to the original in `%LOCALAPPDATA%\SmartFade\captures\`; cited by JSONL `seq`; **there are no pcap frame
numbers and no USBPcap capture**) and the owner's statements of the same afternoon,
[captures/2026-10-30-e8-owner-statements.txt](captures/2026-10-30-e8-owner-statements.txt) (SHA-256
`950607d04edc9377c75f684a72d82bd26e293631986bbdbc9881a1996afd9996`). E8 was an **active** session of our own software under
SC-1 ([safety-checks.md](safety-checks.md)): it sent the poll and the fetch of a listed record and nothing else (no System
write, no data header, no payload, no control transfer, nothing on interface 0); it was **not** a SmartSoft session. Host: the
owner's PC; backend `SmartFade.Api` at commit 08d1284 in `--Device:Mode=Usb`, WinUSB bound with Zadig to interface 1; console
firmware not re-read (a read-only session cannot fetch ConsoleVersion; last reading 3.0.2.9.0.43, F48); power cycles since E3b
UNKNOWN; the show backup is not recorded as specified, there is no action log and USBPcap did not run (H68 untestable). The
owner described his actions afterwards, in his own order and without times, so every time below comes from the console's own
data and each match with an action is INFERRED unless an LCD text names it. Rig: one ADJ DOTZ BAR 1.4 on DMX 5–16, nothing on
DMX IN. Notation as in the E3 and E3b sections: "LED block k" = LedStatus body[1 + 6(k − 1) .. 6k] (phase A the first three
bytes, phase B the last three; steady = phase A equals phase B, blinking = phase A differs from phase B), "FaderInfo record N" =
body[1 + 4(N − 1) .. 4N] with its bytes numbered 0–3 (byte 0 of record 1 is body[1]). **All rows F110–F120 are OBSERVED (one
session, one PC, one show) unless stated, and none is VERIFIED.** *(That is the E8 wording, kept. **Update 2026-10-30, evening:** the second session of our software, E8b, reproduced most of these rows: F110, F112, F113, F114 (the block 53 pattern), F115, F116, F119 and F120 are now VERIFIED and F117, F118 VERIFIED in part; F111 stays OBSERVED. Each row's Status cell says so and carries the E8b frames; VERIFIED here means reproduced behaviour, never clearance for sending.)* The owner's words are OBSERVED for what he saw; Claude's
readings are INFERRED; a verbal confirmation never promotes. **Nothing here clears any message:** SC-1's clearance (the poll

| F117 | **STACK.** At 377.334 s PlaybackInfo record 5 (body[65..80]) bytes 0–1 changed `01 01` → `00 00` (seq 24963; the rest of the record stayed `FF 00 00 00 01 00 01 00 00 32 00 32 00 00`); at 377.392 s the LCD read "Live:14.0 Next: 1.0" / "Mem:01/01 Mem:01/01" (seq 24967); at 377.440 s **LED block 60 went `46 46 00 46 46 00` → `FF FF 00 FF FF 00`** (yellow, full; seq 24971); at 379.076 s the LCD read "Jump to step:> 1" / "Mem:01" (seq 25057); at 379.150 / 379.202 s the LCD and block 60 returned to their earlier states. In REC MEM and REC SEQ block 60 blinks `C8 C8 00 0A 0A 00` (F116), which matches the owner's "STACK is flashing". Manual (printed pp.60–61, DOCUMENTED): pressing STACK activates it (lit at full), the cue stack is loaded onto the Crossfader at step 0 so that GO fades to step 1, and the LCDs show the stack's state; an empty stack stays off when not activated, otherwise it is dimmed; GOTO (held) lets the display scroll to any step. Readings, INFERRED: block 60 is the STACK button's LED (dimmed yellow when inactive with a stored stack of 19 steps, F74; full when active); record 5 bytes 0–1 = 0 is "loaded at step 0" (the LCD's "Next: 1.0" agrees); "Jump to step" is the GOTO display of a second, held press, after which the stack was inactive again. "Live:14.0" is not explained | **VERIFIED 2026-10-30 in part** (E8 + E8b; was OBSERVED (the changes, once)): LED block 60 `46 46 00 46 46 00` → `FF FF 00 FF FF 00` together with the LCD "Live:14.0 Next: 1.0" / "Mem:01/01 Mem:01/01" when STACK is activated (E8: seq 24967, 24971; E8b: the scripted press `go with stack lit` 20:55:45.183, LCD seq 56724, block 60 seq 56728, steady for 51.7 s until STACK was switched off, block 60 dim again at seq 60595), and the short unlogged STACK action at 447.985–448.672 s (seq 22973 … 23015) that repeated E8's three states within 0.68 s (LCD "Live:14.0 Next: 1.0", then "Jump to step:> 1" / "Mem:01", block 60 back to dim). **Not reproduced:** PlaybackInfo record 5 bytes 0–1 `01 01` → `00 00` at the press (they were already `00 00` from the first PlaybackInfo of E8b on, nothing to reset after a power cycle). **E8b annotations (the E8 text is kept):** (1) "Live:14.0" is the stack's last cue (INFERRED): `$SEQUENCE 48` of `SHOW06.ASC` has 19 cues, 1.0 … 9.0, 10.1 … 10.6, 11.0 … 14.0, and cue 14.0 is `$$MEMORY 0 0` = "Mem:01/01"; at step 0 the Live field shows the last cue and the Next field cue 1.0 (the later steps match the file, F130); this resolves E8's '"Live:14.0" is not explained'. (2) "Jump to step" is the GOTO display (manual printed p.61, DOCUMENTED: "Activate GOTO mode (held): As long as the button is held, the display will allow you to scroll to any step. Let go of STACK to transport the whole sequence to this step in Next - ready for the next crossfade."), but E8's reading of it as 'the display of a second, **held** press' is **not supported**: E8b's logged press-and-hold (`stack press` 20:45:07.225 to `stack release` 20:45:15.001, from an inactive stack, block 60 dim) produced no list at all (owner: "if I hold it it does nothing I think, if I just press it and release right after I press it it lit"), and the three states of the unlogged action followed one another within 0.68 s. INFERRED: GOTO needs the stack to be active. (3) The owner's late answer (E8b statements, answer 1, headed "STACK around 20:46:20") is "I pressed it twice back then": two quick presses fit the 0.68-s sequence (activate, then the GOTO display and back to inactive), but the time he names is 49 s after the only unlogged STACK activity in the data (20:45:31, 447.985 s), and the console listed nothing at all between 489.7 s and 504.7 s (20:46:12.8–20:46:27.8, curator check over the log), so nothing was reported at 20:46:20 (497 s): his time is an estimate or the action was another one, and which presses produce the three states stays UNKNOWN (a logged double press settles it, reverse-engineering.md). the guided script's timed action log `2026-10-30-e8b-actions.txt` (SHA-256

of our own software under SC-1 ([safety-checks.md](safety-checks.md)): it sent the poll and the fetch of a listed record
and nothing else (29 799 OUT transfers, all 12-byte headers: 27 484 polls and 2 315 fetches; the bus confirms it: no System
write, no data header, no payload, no control transfer of ours); it was **not** a SmartSoft session. Host: the owner's PC;
backend `SmartFade.Api` at commit c783223 in `--Device:Mode=Usb` for 2026-10-30, WinUSB bound with Zadig to interface 1
**during** the USBPcap capture (root hub `USBPcap1`, INFERRED); the console power-cycled before the first Connect;
firmware not re-read (the owner's Diagnostics reading of the same evening is 3.0.2.9.0.43, F48; a read-only session cannot
fetch ConsoleVersion); show backup `SHOW06.ASC` taken at 20:32:30, byte-identical to `SHOW04.ASC` and `SHOW05.ASC`. Rig:
one DOTZ BAR on DMX 5–16, nothing on DMX IN. Notation as in the E8 section; `t` = seconds since the backend's `captureStart`
(20:38:03.147 local time = 19:38:03.147Z); the backend's log, the action log and the bus share one clock (median offset
0.022 ms, F122). **All rows F122–F130 are OBSERVED (one session, one PC, one show) unless stated, and none is VERIFIED.**
The owner's words are OBSERVED for what he saw or did; Claude's readings are INFERRED; a verbal confirmation never promotes.
**Nothing here clears any message:** SC-1's clearance (the poll and the fetch of a listed record, read-only) is unchanged.

| F122 | **The bus view of a read-only session of our software.** USBPcap ran from before the Zadig bind (20:35:46) to after the session; the console split holds 125 932 frames = 62 968 transfers: 62 957 bulk on interface 1 (29 799 OUT on 0x04, 33 158 IN on 0x83), 7 control on EP0 (three synthetic injected descriptor frames, f1–f6, and four real ones at the bind, F123) and 4 interrupt on 0x81 (F63). **The 61 112 transfers of the backend's log equal the bus byte for byte and in order**, every one with USBD status 0x00000000 (the log's own `status` is null; all 62 957 bulk URBs completed with status 0); every OUT is one URB and our host sent 29 799 12-byte headers on 0x04 and nothing else (nothing on 0x02, no interrupt IN data: the HID interface carried nothing). The log's times and the bus agree within 0.75 ms (backend submission minus bus submission: median −0.022 ms, range −0.753 … −0.005; backend completion minus bus completion: median +0.022 ms, range +0.007 … +3.934), so the action log, the backend's log and the pcap can be compared directly; tshark 4.0.3's `usb.capdata` equals the normalized data in 62 957 of 62 957 data-carrying bulk records. The split is complete (the k-th address-10 frame of the raw capture is frame k of the split for every k, 125 932 of 125 932). **WinUSB splits long reads:** every IN read longer than 64 bytes and not a multiple of 64 is **two URBs on the bus**, a whole-packet part (64, 64, 192, 448 or 512 bytes) and the remainder (22, 26, 8, 8 or 7 bytes): 1 845 reads (PlaybackInfo 86 bytes × 1 305, Independents 90 × 4, FaderInfo 200 × 151, LedStatus 456 × 97, OutputLevels 519 × 288), the concatenation equal to the backend's record in 1 845 of 1 845 (example: the LedStatus read seq 9 is f36 with 448 bytes plus f38 with 8 bytes); this explains 33 158 bulk IN URBs against the log's 31 313 reads. INFERRED: a host-side behaviour of WinUSB (SmartSoft's ETC driver read every payload in one URB in E2–E3b, F51); the console sees IN tokens either way and no console-side difference is expected. No zero-length packet on 0x83 and no read whose length is a multiple of 64, so H75 is not tested by this | OBSERVED (one session; the log against the bus is a second evidence channel for the same transfers); the WinUSB split INFERRED as host-side behaviour | f19 … f125932 of the console split; seq 1 … 61112; seq 9 = f36 + f38; [E8b record](captures/2026-10-30-e8b-second-read-only-session.md) "USBPcap: the bus view of our session" and "Completeness of the split"; `tools/protocol-analyzer/usbpcap_to_jsonl.py` (normalizer version 2, 0 other-device frames); F51, F63, F65; H68, H75 |

## Our console: SmartSoft writing to the console, the first PC → console writes (E9, 2026-10-30, late evening)

Source for every row: [captures/2026-10-30-e9-smartsoft-writes.md](captures/2026-10-30-e9-smartsoft-writes.md)
(record), the committed console-only USBPcap split `2026-10-30-e9-smartsoft-writes.pcap` (device address 10 of the
root-hub capture, SHA-256 `a5a24de9ef31b2b59de5f3ed72b1415129fd79c4be39bccb196b765eb7536d25`, 214 494 frames,
22:19:59.957–22:48:44.113 local time; cited by `fN`, the frame number of the split; the raw root-hub capture holds other
devices and is not committed) with its normalization `2026-10-30-e9-smartsoft-writes.jsonl` (SHA-256
`79a1a2dc26e461f7fdd50917cf9713af613a17eaa5c6be393b575b77822f4edd`, cited by JSONL `seq`), the guided script's timed action
log `2026-10-30-e9-actions.txt` (SHA-256 `aeddfdddecfca211bed5484f8d6d492a4dda12c6704a3ac83425c6921ec26ccd`) and the owner's
statements [captures/2026-10-30-e9-owner-statements.txt](captures/2026-10-30-e9-owner-statements.txt) (SHA-256
`88b92256e275e433963213926387d27686541f70b6820b2b364853b72766f53c`, 52 lines: the owner's answers of 2026-10-31 to the
record's questions were appended after the record was written, whose evidence table lists the earlier 3 394-byte version
`be5eb099803c23bf4bc11c8a7ef692dc04d77ed7f112110b092e5e1734a64ac2`). E9 was a **SmartSoft** session: the owner drove SmartSoft
3.0.43 (package 3.0.2.9.0.43b), Online, through its Console Simulator with `tools/usb-probe/e9-guided-session.ps1`, and USBPcap
recorded it passively. **Our own software sent nothing; every write below is SmartSoft's, made as for any user.** Host: the
owner's PC (Windows 11 Pro build 26300, Memory Integrity off), interface 1 on ETC's driver (service ETCHSP), console firmware
3.0.2.9.0.43 (the session's ConsoleVersion object), not power-cycled since E8b, show backed up first (`SHOW07.ASC` at 22:18:26,
byte-identical to `SHOW04.ASC` … `SHOW06.ASC`); rig: the DOTZ BAR on DMX 5–16, nothing on DMX IN. The session changed show data
on the console (F139). Notation: `seqN` is the JSONL record number, `fN` a frame of the split (the data-carrying frame: the
submission frame for OUT, the completion frame for IN); a write is cited as header / payload; `pseq` is a payload's own sequence
byte; "body[n]", "LED block k" (LedStatus body[1 + 6(k − 1) .. 6k], phase A the first three bytes, phase B the last three),
"FaderInfo record N" and the LCD cells as in the E8 and E8b sections; ConsoleEvent bodies are written
`[kind][index or code][value][previous]`; a Memory object is named by its fetch qualifiers (a, b), memory number 24a + b + 1,
and a System record by (a, b, c). **All rows are OBSERVED (one session, one PC, one show) unless stated, and none is VERIFIED**
(the second session is E9b, proposed in [reverse-engineering.md](reverse-engineering.md)). **Nothing here clears any message:**
SC-1's clearance (poll and listed fetch, read-only) is unchanged, no type-1 header is encoded anywhere in the solution *(Superseded 2026-10-31, later the same day: the owner implemented SC-2's whitelist in `HspOutbound` and `HspReadSession`, sent only by a session with live control switched on for the day (`Device:Usb:LiveControlDate`); reviewed under SC-2 item 10, NEEDS CHANGES at the first pass, see safety-checks.md; nothing is cleared for sending before E11 on the owner's go.)*, and the
write messages below are the input of a future safety check SC-2 (the record's "Input for a future safety check (SC-2)").
The owner's words are OBSERVED for what he saw or did; his answers of 2026-10-31 and Claude's readings never promote.

**Update 2026-10-31 (E9b, [E9b record](captures/2026-10-31-e9b-smartsoft-writes-repeat.md)).** The second session (the repeat of E9: three SmartSoft sessions in one capture, 427 data
writes) reproduced the rows below. F131, F132, F133, F134, F138 and F140 are now **VERIFIED** for the scopes named in their Status
cells, F135 and F136 **VERIFIED in part**, F137 and F139 stay OBSERVED with an E9b annotation (F137: E9b had no record with c ≠ 0),
and every Source cell carries an "E9b" entry; E9b's own new facts F141–F147 are in the next section. "All rows are OBSERVED … none
is VERIFIED" above is the E9 wording, kept. **VERIFIED here says that SmartSoft and the console were seen to behave so in two

| F136 | **Record functions from the PC, and what the console lists.** (a) REC MEM (key 0x41) with channel 5 at 0x79 on the physical fader, then bump 9 (key 0x08): the console stored the live look (channel 5 = 0x79, all other levels 0, an empty label block) and **listed the new Memory object by itself** (list [Memory (a = 0, b = 8), 22, 21], seq 74003; body `00 08 01 60` + levels + label block, seq 74007, f148014) within 18–98 ms of the storing key, then returned to the channel view; (b) EDIT MEM (0x42) from memory mode, then a bump (0x08): the console switched to channel mode (ConsoleStatus body[0] → 0, seq 77858), listed **System (39) (5, 5, slot − 1)** with body `05 05 08` (seq 77862), showed the memory's levels on the channel bumps (LED block 5 G = 0x79) and the LCD "Edit:" / "01/09" (seq 77878) with no level object changing (blind); a second EDIT MEM stored (the Memory object was listed with the edited level, LCD "SmartFade Menu" on line 2 for about 1.4 s); (c) CLEAR (0x38) inside the edit cancelled it (list [23, 14, 21, 22], seq 80941; the previous mode restored; **no Memory listed**); (d) COPY (0x3E), a source bump (0x08) and a target bump (0x09): LCD "Copy Memory/Seq" / "Select source", then "Copy 01/09" / "Select target" (seq 83198), and the console listed **Memory (a = 0, b = 9)** with memory 9's levels and label (seq 84664 / 84668). An EDIT MEM session started from the stored levels whatever the physical faders' positions, so "EDIT MEM, bump, CLEAR with all faders at zero" deleted nothing (the guided script's deletion steps, seq 91071 … 96083: System (5, 5, 8) and (5, 5, 9) listed, LED block 5 G = 0x79 and no Memory listed after CLEAR). The two Mem Edit steps went through the console's EDIT MEM by Simulator key and fader events; the second one, which pressed bump 7 (key 0x06) after EDIT MEM, edited **memory 7** (LCD "Edit:" / "01/07", seq 68364; Memory (a = 0, b = 6) listed with channel 7 = 0xFF, seq 68832), the first memory 1 (Memory (a = 0, b = 0) with channel 7 = 0x7F, seq 67552). **repeat of E9**, again a **SmartSoft** session: three SmartSoft Online sessions in one capture (JSONL seq 4 … 64680, 64681 …
70067, 70068 … 104438; SmartSoft 3.0.43, package 3.0.2.9.0.43b), driven by the owner through its Console Simulator with
`tools/usb-probe/e9b-guided-session.ps1` (3ce3900) after a console power cycle, recorded passively by USBPcap. **Our own software
sent nothing; every write below is SmartSoft's, made as for any user.** Host: the owner's PC (Windows 11 Pro build 26300, Memory
Integrity off), interface 1 on ETC's driver (service ETCHSP), console firmware 3.0.2.9.0.43 (the sessions' ConsoleVersion
objects), the show backed up first (`SHOW08.ASC` at 08:35:31, before the capture; it still carried E9's mistaken edit of memory
7, F139); rig: the DOTZ BAR on DMX 5–16, nothing on DMX IN. Notation as in the E9 section. **All rows are OBSERVED (one session,
one PC, one show) unless stated.** **Nothing here clears any message:** SC-1's clearance (poll and listed fetch, read-only) is
unchanged, no type-1 header is encoded anywhere in the solution *(Superseded 2026-10-31, later the same day: the owner implemented SC-2's whitelist in `HspOutbound` and `HspReadSession`, sent only by a session with live control switched on for the day (`Device:Usb:LiveControlDate`); reviewed under SC-2 item 10, NEEDS CHANGES at the first pass, see safety-checks.md; nothing is cleared for sending before E11 on the owner's go.)*, and the write messages below are the input of the future SC-2

| F141 | **The last mover wins between a PC fader and the physical fader (channel mode, both directions).** (1) *A PC event after the physical fader:* a kind-0 event sets the control at once, from its first event, whatever the physical fader's position and the console's level. Fader 1 (physical fader back at zero, record 1 = 0x00, SmartSoft's own fader 1 still at 0xFF): the Simulator's drag down started at `FC FF`, and the first samples after it read 0xF2 (DMX 1, seq 39741, 650.423 s) and 0xEC (record 1, seq 39747, f79494), the level jumping from 0x00 to the PC's value and following it down. Fader 5 (physical fader at 0x84, seq 42704): the Simulator's fader 5 from zero took record 5 from 0x84 to 0x0D at once (DMX 5 = 0x0A at seq 42968; record 5 = 0x0D at seq 42974, f85948, 698.716 s) and then followed the PC up to 0xFF. The MASTER (at 0x86 from the console's own MASTER, seq 76687): the drag `30 FD 00` (previous byte 0x00) took body[194] from 0x86 to 0xF3 at once (seq 82071, f164142), then 0xD9, 0xE9 and 0xFF (seq 82161), block 53 steady again (seq 82179). (2) *The physical fader after a PC event:* the PC's fader 1 stood at 0xFF (pseq 231, 521.468 s) with the physical fader 1 at zero, and record 1 reported 0xFF for 23 s (521.5–544.7 s) with the bump LED steady; when the owner moved the physical fader up, record 1 dropped **0xFF → 0x0D at the first registered movement** (DMX 1 = 0x09, seq 32846; record 1 at seq 32850, f65700, 544.800 s) and then followed the hand to 0x82 (seq 32999), with no write. Fader 5 likewise: the PC's fader 5 at 0x00, the physical fader 5 still at about half; record 5 went **0x00 → 0x83** at the first registered movement (seq 45477, f90954, 734.839 s) and then followed the hand down to 0x00 (seq 45595). **The physical fader takes over at once from its own position without reaching the PC's level first, and a PC event takes over at once from the console's level: no pick-up in channel mode, and the bump LED did not blink in either direction.** The owner's answers agree ("it instantly go to the value of the physical fader", "h81 pickup = at once"; statements). **SmartSoft's Simulator does not follow the console** (INFERRED from three cases): its drags started from its own last position (fader 1 from 0xFF at 650.3 s while the console stood at 0x00; fader 5 from 0x00 at 698.5 s while the console stood at 0x84; the MASTER from about full at 1326.4 s while the console stood at 0x86), so the first event of a drag can make the output jump (H89). **The capture ended in a split state:** the console's MASTER level was the PC's 0xFF from 1327.4 s to the end (body[194] = 0xFF in all 29 FaderInfo payloads from seq 82161 to seq 95285) while the physical MASTER stood where the owner had left it in H85, about half (0x86; INFERRED, the faders are not motorised); the owner noticed "about 50 %" after the session and had it at 100 % again about 09:35 (statements, in no capture). Relevance: neither side is told when the other overrides it; a sender learns the console's level only from FaderInfo, and the physical position is not reported until the fader is moved, so a UI must show the console's reported level and never assume the physical position. ## Our console: SmartSoft editing the patch and the stack / sequences (E12, 2026-10-31, afternoon)

Source for every row: [captures/2026-10-31-e12-smartsoft-patch-stack-edits.md](captures/2026-10-31-e12-smartsoft-patch-stack-edits.md)
(record, with the `capture-analyst` decoding of the same evening, on Opus 5.5 at the owner's request), the committed console-only
USBPcap split `2026-10-31-e12-smartsoft-patch-stack-edits.pcap` (device address 27 of the root-hub capture, SHA-256
`63b32fc153a9a85b7c327e60efb217b42ae6a1dc552093527cb16672fe421094`, 397 536 frames, 15:39:43.890770–16:32:14.523094 local time;
cited by `fN`, the frame number of the split, for OUT the submission frame and for IN the completion frame; the raw root-hub capture
holds other devices and is not committed) with its normalization `2026-10-31-e12-smartsoft-patch-stack-edits.jsonl` (SHA-256
`e8a91f7451a640f4f1ae5470aab7366c3479cfd9a2a0e3425ca56e2cfde41685`, cited by JSONL `seq`), the guided script's timed action log
`2026-10-31-e12-actions.txt` (SHA-256 `2ae5e4045f5834deadd9c5a3549b82d276cfb6ab14f67a8586b384cabb136800`) and the owner's statements
[captures/2026-10-31-e12-owner-statements.txt](captures/2026-10-31-e12-owner-statements.txt) (SHA-256
`88166cb97374f5b5fb88313de6f1f1af7e511f81cf58c16c2fe20b8d2fe128a8`). E12 was a **SmartSoft** session: three SmartSoft Online sessions in
one capture (JSONL seq 6 … 138843, 138845 … 194103, 194105 … 198768; SmartSoft 3.0.43, package 3.0.2.9.0.43b), in which the owner, led
by `tools/usb-probe/e12-guided-session.ps1` (7f91015) after a console power cycle, edited the patch, IND 1, the stack, the sequence
KOTLIK, memory 9 and page names in SmartSoft's tabs and used the console's own patch and step-timing menus (step H), restoring every
change; USBPcap recorded it passively. **Our own software sent nothing; every write below is SmartSoft's, made as for any user.** Host:
the owner's PC (Windows 11 Pro build 26300, Memory Integrity off), interface 1 on ETC's driver (service ETCHSP), console firmware
3.0.2.9.0.43 (the sessions' ConsoleVersion objects, seq 99, 138933, 194193), the show backed up first (`SHOW12.ASC`) and saved after the
session (`SHOW13.ASC`, byte-identical); rig: four RGB fixtures on DMX 5–16, DMX 100–102 unconnected. A write is cited as **Wn**, the
n-th of the 70 data writes in capture order, with its header / payload seq and payload frame. **All rows are OBSERVED (one capture,
three SmartSoft sessions, one PC, one show) unless stated, and none is VERIFIED** (the repeat is E12b, proposed in
[reverse-engineering.md](reverse-engineering.md)). *(Superseded in part 2026-11-01 by E12b, text kept: the repeat ran on 2026-10-31,
late evening ([E12b record](captures/2026-10-31-e12b-smartsoft-patch-stack-edits-repeat.md)); F151, F152, F156 and F157 are now
VERIFIED and F153, F154 and F158 VERIFIED in part (E12 + E12b; the scopes are in the rows), F155 and F159 stay OBSERVED, and E12b's
new facts F160–F165 are in the next section.)* **Nothing here clears any message:** SC-1's clearance (poll and listed fetch,
read-only) is unchanged; SC-2's classes are unchanged (the Patch and Independents writes class F, "Never"; the Memory write class D,
NO-GO); the step commands and the Sequence, SequenceMain, StepContent and Page writes are in no SC-2 class; no encoder for any of them
exists in the solution (`HspOutbound` encodes ConsoleEvent only), and our code never sends them. They change show data and are
reversible only by another exact write or by the SD backup. The owner's words are OBSERVED for what he saw or did; his answers and the
analysts' readings never promote.

| # | Fact | Status | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| F151 | **The step commands as SmartSoft sends them, and the console's answer.** InsertStep (32): W6, W17, W19, W28, W30; DeleteStep (34): W8, W57, W60, W61, W65; MoveStep (35): W58; id 42 (no name in the engine's table, F15): W59. Ids 32, 34 and 42 have the header `01 00 08 00` + stale words (F52) and a 4-byte body `[a][b][u16 BE c]`; MoveStep has `01 00 07 00` and a 3-byte body `[a][b][c]` (example W6: payload seq 30121 (f60241) `05 04 00 20 30 13 00 96`). **a** = 0x30 = 48 for the stack (10 of 10) and 0 for KOTLIK (2 of 2). **InsertStep:** b = the index the new step takes, c = its cue number × 10 for the stack (4 of 4: `30 13 00 96` = index 19, 15.0, twice; `30 11 00 79` = index 17, 12.1; `30 14 00 8D` = index 20, 14.1); for KOTLIK `00 06 00 50` = index 6 with c = 8.0, while the new step became 7.0 (c's meaning for a sequence UNKNOWN, H103). **DeleteStep:** b = the index, c = 0 (`30 13 00 00`, `00 06 00 00`, `30 14 00 00` twice, `30 13 00 00`). **MoveStep** `30 11 14`: b = 17 (from), c = 20 (the index the step holds after the move); **id 42** `30 14 00 8E`, 47 ms later: index 20 became cue 14.2, which the owner logged (INFERRED from this one sample: id 42 renumbers a step). No id 33 (InsertPointQStep): the point cue 12.1 was an InsertStep with c = 12.1; ids 29–31 appeared in neither direction. ## MIDI

| # | Fact | Status | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| F20 | The console implements Control Change, Program Change and Note On/Off on one user-set channel, plus MIDI Show Control (fw 3.0) | DOCUMENTED | [M15], [M30] |
| F21 | The MIDI spec v1.5 and the console manual v3.0 **disagree** on PC 124–127 and on the CC 124/125 names | DOCUMENTED contradiction | midi-spec-analysis.md |
| F22 | MIDI provides no acknowledgements and no state readback | DOCUMENTED | [M15] |
